## Live-window consumer audit

The live window changed while this dispatch was running: two concurrent Sobolev supplier edits introduced 13 direct consumers outside this run. These specific current uses were read and reviewed without editing those carriers. The current two supplier Statements, their exact n≥2 and extension-domain hypotheses, and all 13 citing F clauses and their proof applications were checked. In particular the two n=1 interior-smoothness examples have separate interval arguments, and the compactness proofs use strict Hölder/critical exponents. This review is limited to these consumed supplier interfaces.

- `cex-smooth-interior-data-do-not-repair-incompatible-dirichlet-corner-values`: still-licensed. F2 and Counterexample 2.1 use k=2,p=2,n=2 on the square. F2 supplies an explicit bounded H² extension with matched values and normal derivatives, so kp>n and any 0<α<1 give the required continuous closure representative. The corner contradiction uses only continuity, not a classical derivative at the corner.
- `cor-smooth-data-give-smooth-interior-solutions`: still-licensed. F3/Proof 2.1 use p=2 and s>m+n/2 on a bounded smooth ball, selecting 0<α<min(1,s−m−n/2). The supplier’s n≥2 is respected; the n=1 branch uses the separate absolutely-continuous representative fact F4. Continuous representatives of the same a.e. class agree on overlaps.
- `cor-smooth-weak-dirichlet-solutions-are-classical`: still-licensed. F2/Proof 2.1 use the smooth-domain extension at each m and choose m>ℓ+n/2, giving continuous derivatives through ℓ on the closure. n≥2 and AC are explicit; the trace conclusion is proved separately. No finite W²,p embedding into C²,α is asserted.
- `cor-sobolev-algebra-above-the-critical-index`: still-licensed. F1 extends each original W^{k,p} function once and applies the supplier to its derivatives on a smooth support ball, avoiding a nonexistent lower-order extension operator on Ω. Proof 1.1 uses the subcritical, critical and bounded cases with s_i=k−|γ_i|. The critical finite exponent 2n and s_i≥1 make the displayed reciprocal-exponent sum at most 1/p; when s_i=0 the original L^p bound is used. kp>n and finite-measure inclusion justify the product bound.
- `cor-w-two-p-regularity-implies-classical-or-holder-regularity-when-p-is-large`: still-licensed. F2/Proofs 1.1 and 2.1–2.3 apply k=2,q=p on the expressly W²,p extension domain. The strict inequalities γ<min(1,2−n/p), 1+γ<2−n/p and p>n/(1−α) match the supplier. The (x_1)_+² caveat correctly excludes a general C²,α conclusion.
- `ex-bootstrapping-a-smooth-poisson-problem`: still-licensed. F4/Verification 2.1 apply p=2 and s>m+n/2 on a slightly larger smooth ball, giving a C^{m,α} representative for n≥2. The n=1 branch is explicit and uses interval absolutely-continuous representatives and the fundamental theorem, not the higher-order supplier outside its dimension range.
- `thm-critical-sobolev-embedding-into-every-finite-lq`: still-licensed. F2/Proof 1.2 take s=nq/(n+q) when q>n, so n/2<s<n, s≥1 and s*=q. The Statement supplies the W^{1,s} extension property for every such s; the subcritical supplier therefore applies at its upper endpoint q=s*. Finite-measure Hölder controls W^{1,s} by W^{1,n}. The q≤n branch uses finite-measure inclusion and no L∞ endpoint is claimed.
- `thm-global-schauder-regularity-for-the-weak-dirichlet-laplacian`: still-licensed. F5/Proof 4.1 use k=2 on the bounded C^{2,α} extension domain and the finite list q_i≥2, q_i≤p. The subcritical exponent nq_i/(n−2q_i), critical finite-L^p branch, and supercritical bound exactly match the supplier. The final p>n gives only C^{1,γ}, γ<1−n/p; the C^{2,α} conclusion comes from the separate Schauder argument.
- `thm-higher-order-rellich-kondrachov`: still-licensed. F3/Proofs 1.1–3.1 apply the supplier to D^α(ηEu_j) on a smooth support ball, with remaining order at least k−m. Subcritical compactness retains q strictly below the critical exponent; the critical branch chooses finite r>q. Hölder compactness chooses β<β′<min(1,k−m−n/p), never the continuous endpoint. Empty Ω is treated separately.
- `thm-rellich-kondrachov-at-the-critical-source-exponent`: still-licensed. F2/Proof 1.1 use k=1,p=n,n≥2 and a finite q′>max(q,n). The critical supplier supplies that uniform finite-L^{q′} bound; interpolation with L^n convergence is licensed and no L∞ compactness is asserted. Empty Ω is separate.
- `thm-rellich-kondrachov-for-p-less-than-n`: still-licensed. F2/Proofs 1.1 and 3.1 apply the subcritical supplier at q=p*=np/(n−p), within its p≤q≤p* range, for n≥2 and 1≤p<n. The target compact exponent is strictly smaller; q<p uses finite-measure inclusion separately. Endpoint p=1 is permitted by the supplier and compactness at p* is not asserted.
- `thm-sobolev-poincare-on-bounded-connected-extension-domains`: still-licensed. F3/Proof 2.1 apply the subcritical bound to v=u−u_Ω at q=p*, with n≥2,1<p<n and the stated W^{1,p} extension domain. The gradient-only estimate uses the separate connected-domain mean-zero Poincaré bound, so the supplier is used only for its full W^{1,p} norm estimate.
- `thm-weak-global-w-two-p-dirichlet-regularity-for-the-laplacian`: still-licensed. F3/Proofs 1.1 and 3.1 start at k=1,q=2, treating n=2 critically, then use k=2 at q_i≥2. For 2q_i<n the next exponent is the subcritical endpoint, for equality every finite p is available, and for 2q_i>n boundedness gives L^p. The finite reciprocal decrement and p>n terminate the bootstrap. The domain supplies the extension property at the displayed orders and no estimate is applied before W²,q membership.
